     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+

 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+// MPI ranks execute the same control flow.  Keep benchmark output single-copy.

+int rankPrintf(const char* format, ...) {
+    if (g_mpiRank != 0) return 0;
+    va_list args;
+    va_start(args, format);
+    const int result = vprintf(format, args);
+    va_end(args);
+    return result;

+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    std::abort();
+}

+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) do { const cudaError_t _cudaError = (operation); \

@@ -541,6 +568,119 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+

codex
The program builds and runs successfully with both one and two MPI ranks. I also verified that the rank-partitioned CUDA timestep exchange preserves validation; I’m making two small cluster-readiness refinements now: select GPUs by node-local rank and avoid launching an empty CUDA grid when ranks outnumber rows.

+// MPI ranks execute the same control flow.  Keep benchmark output single-copy.
+int g_mpiRank = 0;
+int g_mpiSize = 1;
+int rankPrintf(const char* format, ...) {
+    if (g_mpiRank != 0) return 0;
+    va_list args;
+    va_start(args, format);
+    const int result = vprintf(format, args);
+    va_end(args);
+    return result;
+}
+#define printf rankPrintf
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) do { const cudaError_t _cudaError = (operation); \
+    if (_cudaError != cudaSuccess) cudaFail(_cudaError, #operation); } while (false)

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
+    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &providedThreadLevel);
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_mpiRank);

+    int deviceCount = 0;

+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+

+// MPI ranks execute the same control flow.  Keep benchmark output single-copy.
+int g_mpiRank = 0;
+int g_mpiSize = 1;
+int rankPrintf(const char* format, ...) {
+    if (g_mpiRank != 0) return 0;
+    va_list args;
+    va_start(args, format);
+    const int result = vprintf(format, args);
+    va_end(args);
+    return result;

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    std::abort();
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);

+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}

+// MPI ranks execute the same control flow.  Keep benchmark output single-copy.

+int rankPrintf(const char* format, ...) {

+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+// MPI ranks execute the same control flow.  Keep benchmark output single-copy.

+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+

 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+int rankPrintf(const char* format, ...) {
+    if (g_mpiRank != 0) return 0;
+    va_list args;
+    va_start(args, format);
+    const int result = vprintf(format, args);
+    va_end(args);
+    return result;
+}
+#define printf rankPrintf
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) do { const cudaError_t _cudaError = (operation); \
+    if (_cudaError != cudaSuccess) cudaFail(_cudaError, #operation); } while (false)
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Types and Constants
 // ============================================================================
@@ -541,6 +568,119 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}

+// MPI ranks execute the same control flow.  Keep benchmark output single-copy.
+int g_mpiRank = 0;

+int rankPrintf(const char* format, ...) {
+    if (g_mpiRank != 0) return 0;
+    va_list args;
+    va_start(args, format);
+    const int result = vprintf(format, args);
+    va_end(args);

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    std::abort();

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+

 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+// MPI ranks execute the same control flow.  Keep benchmark output single-copy.

+int rankPrintf(const char* format, ...) {
+    if (g_mpiRank != 0) return 0;

+    const int result = vprintf(format, args);

+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    std::abort();
+}

+    if (_cudaError != cudaSuccess) cudaFail(_cudaError, #operation); } while (false)

@@ -541,6 +568,119 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+

 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+// MPI ranks execute the same control flow.  Keep benchmark output single-copy.
+int g_mpiRank = 0;
+int g_mpiSize = 1;
+int rankPrintf(const char* format, ...) {
+    if (g_mpiRank != 0) return 0;
+    va_list args;
+    va_start(args, format);
+    const int result = vprintf(format, args);
+    va_end(args);
+    return result;

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    std::abort();
+}

+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) do { const cudaError_t _cudaError = (operation); \
+    if (_cudaError != cudaSuccess) cudaFail(_cudaError, #operation); } while (false)

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[time * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+

 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+// MPI ranks execute the same control flow.  Keep benchmark output single-copy.
+int g_mpiRank = 0;
+int g_mpiSize = 1;
+int rankPrintf(const char* format, ...) {
+    if (g_mpiRank != 0) return 0;
+    va_list args;
+    va_start(args, format);
+    const int result = vprintf(format, args);
+    va_end(args);

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFail(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) do { const cudaError_t _cudaError = (operation); \
+    if (_cudaError != cudaSuccess) cudaFail(_cudaError, #operation); } while (false)

@@ -541,6 +568,119 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The complete history is resident on every accelerator.  Rows are owned by MPI
+// ranks, so only the newly produced row needs exchanging after each timestep.
+struct CudaState {
+    val_t* kij = nullptr;
+    int* tau = nullptr;
+    val_t* areas = nullptr;
+    val_t* rho = nullptr;
+    val_t* radE = nullptr;
+    val_t* radB = nullptr;
+    val_t* distances = nullptr;
+
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&kij, n * n * sizeof(*kij)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&tau, n * n * sizeof(*tau)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&areas, n * sizeof(*areas)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&rho, n * sizeof(*rho)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radE, tn * sizeof(*radE)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&radB, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&distances, n * sizeof(*distances)));
+    }
+
+    void uploadStatic(const SimulationState& state) const {
+        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
+        const size_t tn = n * state.numTimesteps;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kij, state.kij.data(), n * n * sizeof(*kij), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(tau, state.tau.data(), n * n * sizeof(*tau), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(areas, state.areas.data(), n * sizeof(*areas), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(rho, state.rho.data(), n * sizeof(*rho), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(radE, state.radE.data(), tn * sizeof(*radE), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(radB, 0, tn * sizeof(*radB)));
+    }
+
+    void release() {
+        if (kij) cudaFree(kij);
+        if (tau) cudaFree(tau);
+        if (areas) cudaFree(areas);
+        if (rho) cudaFree(rho);
+        if (radE) cudaFree(radE);
+        if (radB) cudaFree(radB);
+        if (distances) cudaFree(distances);
+        *this = {};
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t timestep, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<size_t>(delay) > timestep) continue;
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        const val_t source = radB[(timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > ZERO) sumB += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+    }
+    radB[timestep * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[timestep * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t sourceIndex, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rowCount) return;
+    const size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorrelation = ZERO;
+    size_t bestTime = 0;
+    for (size_t delay = 0; delay < timesteps; ++delay) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t time = delay; time < timesteps; ++time)
+            sum += radB[(time - delay) * n + sourceIndex] * radB[time * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorrelation) {
+            maxCorrelation = sum;
+            bestTime = delay;
+        }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTime);
+}
+
+void rowPartition(size_t rows, size_t& firstRow, size_t& rowCount) {
+    const size_t base = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    const size_t remainder = rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    rowCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    firstRow = base * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) +
+               std::min(static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank), remainder);
+}
+
+std::vector<int> rowCounts(size_t rows, size_t valuesPerRow) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < g_mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = rows / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rows % static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count * valuesPerRow > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI count exceeds INT_MAX\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count * valuesPerRow);
+    }
+    return counts;
+}
+
+std::vector<int> displacements(const std::vector<int>& counts) {
+    std::vector<int> result(counts.size(), 0);
+    for (size_t i = 1; i < counts.size(); ++i) result[i] = result[i - 1] + counts[i - 1];
+    return result;
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================
